Method: 研究采用了以下方法:(1)针对诚实但好奇场景,建立有限码长的逆向界(converse bounds)和可达界(achievability bounds);(2)针对恶意场景,设计了结合交互式哈希和信道输出接收测试的构造方案;(3)将每个输入分布到多个量子比特上,使得擦除能够对不诚实接收者完全隐藏整个输入,即使输入与外部参考系统纠缠;(4)推导固定错误率的渐近展开式,比较两种安全场景的容量。

Result: 研究获得以下结果:(1)在诚实但好奇场景下,有限码长的逆向界和可达界相匹配;(2)成功构造了针对恶意行为的安全协议,能够抵抗不诚实接收者的攻击;(3)推导出固定错误率下的渐近展开式;(4)证明了恶意场景和诚实但好奇场景具有相同的容量,表明额外的安全要求不会降低协议的渐近传输效率。

Conclusion: 本文在信息论安全框架下完整刻画了量子擦除信道上量子态不经意传输的容量,证明了在诚实但好奇和恶意两种安全模型下协议具有相同的渐近容量。研究提供了有限码长下的紧界,并提出了实用的恶意安全构造方案,为量子密码协议在有噪声信道上的实现提供了理论基础。这表明即使面对更强的安全威胁,通过适当的协议设计也不会牺牲渐近传输效率。

Abstract: We study quantum-state oblivious transfer over the quantum erasure channel under information-theoretic security guarantees. Specifically, Alice holds two quantum systems, Bob wishes to recover one of them, and the parties have access to independent channel uses and authenticated public classical communication, with no initial shared entanglement. We consider both honest-but-curious and malicious behavior. We establish finite-blocklength converse and achievability bounds, which match in the honest-but-curious setting. The malicious setting construction combines interactive hashing with tests of whether Bob received selected channel outputs, and distributes each input across several qubits so that erasures conceal an entire input from a dishonest receiver, even when the inputs are entangled with an external reference. We also derive fixed-error asymptotic expansions and show that both malicious and honest-but-curious settings have the same capacity.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文发展了一套统一的稳定子码理论框架,将量子比特、量子多能级系统(qudit)、振子和平面转子系统的稳定子码统一处理,通过量子相空间中的广义格结构构造了多种混合系统的量子纠错码,并给出了逻辑算符、Clifford-Gaussian门、码距离和最优解码的完整理论描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稳定子码理论主要局限于单一类型的量子系统(如纯量子比特或纯振子系统),缺乏统一处理混合量子系统(振子-转子-量子比特混合系统)的理论框架。需要建立一个能够处理commuting Pauli算符、振子位移和平面转子位移的广义稳定子码理论,以实现更灵活的量子纠错方案和跨系统的码构造。

Method: 1) 基于Rieffel的非交换几何结果,在量子相空间中构造广义格结构作为稳定子群,保证有限逻辑维度;2) 通过将给定稳定子码与Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP)码级联,应用传统格理论结果后再解级联的技术;3) 从辛对偶格导出逻辑算符的解析形式;4) 从格对称性确定Clifford-Gaussian门;5) 构造振子-转子、转子-qudit、振子-转子-量子比特等无法通过广义Clifford变换分解的混合码;6) 引入基于部分提升Golay码的振子-量子比特码(对Golay码一半坐标应用Construction A);7) 将症状子空间组织为向量丛结构;8) 构造有限能量码字,其编码是等距映射直到指数小误差。

Result: 1) 成功构造了多种不可分解的混合系统稳定子码(振子-转子码、转子-qudit码、振子-转子-量子比特码);2) 证明码维度等于稳定子格的covolume;3) 导出了逻辑算符、Clifford-Gaussian门的解析表达式;4) 建立了统一的码距离、最优解码和量子权重枚举子理论,恢复了Pauli、qudit和GKP版本以及已知的MacWilliams恒等式;5) 构造的有限能量码字的编码误差随阻尼参数倒数指数衰减;6) 成功构造了基于部分提升Golay码的新型振子-量子比特码。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个统一的广义稳定子码理论框架,通过量子相空间中的格结构成功地将qudit Pauli码、振子位移码和转子位移码纳入同一理论体系。该框架不仅能够系统地处理混合量子系统的纠错码构造,还提供了逻辑算符、Clifford-Gaussian门、码距离和解码的完整理论工具。通过与GKP码的级联-解级联技术,可以有效利用经典格理论结果。构造的有限能量码字具有实用价值,为混合量子系统的容错量子计算提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We develop a theory of stabilizer codes whose stabilizer groups consist of commuting qudit Paulis, oscillator displacements, and/or planar-rotor displacements. We consider codes whose stabilizer groups form generalized lattices in quantum phase space, a condition that guarantees a finite logical dimension. We construct oscillator-rotor, rotor-qudit, and oscillator-rotor-qubit codes that cannot be decomposed into separate subsystems by generalized Clifford transformations. We also introduce an oscillator-qubit code arising from a partially lifted Golay code, where Construction A is applied to half of the Golay code coordinates. We derive analytical forms of logical operators from the symplectic dual lattice, determine Clifford-Gaussian gates from lattice symmetries, show that the code dimension equals the covolume of the stabilizer lattice, and organize the syndrome subspaces into a vector bundle. Our technique builds on earlier non-commutative geometric results by Rieffel, but it can often be interpreted simply as concatenating a given stabilizer code with a Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) code, applying conventional lattice-theoretic results, and unconcatenating. We formulate distances, optimal decoding, and quantum weight enumerators, recovering the Pauli, qudit, and GKP versions together with known MacWilliams identities. We construct finite-energy codewords whose encoding is an isometry up to an error exponentially small in the inverse damping parameter.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出了"拓扑无关量子优势"的概念,证明在通信网络中量子协议可以优于所有经典协议而不依赖于特定的网络拓扑结构,并通过三方和四方网络场景完整刻画了经典关联多面体,揭示了多个拓扑无关量子优势实例,包括能够实现半设备无关的非幺正量子信道认证和序列量子网络拓扑认证。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量子通信优势研究通常建立在固定的通信网络基础上,缺乏对不同网络拓扑结构的普适性。研究者希望建立一种新的量子优势形式,使其不依赖于特定的通信拓扑配置,即使经典方可以任意分配通信资源和共享无限经典随机性,量子协议仍能保持优势,从而揭示量子通信在网络场景中更本质和鲁棒的优越性。

Result: 研究完整刻画了最小三方场景中的经典关联多面体,其面不等式揭示了多个拓扑无关量子优势实例。其中一个不等式实现了非幺正量子信道的半设备无关认证,另一个不等式在无需预先假设网络配置的情况下认证了序列量子网络拓扑。在四方网络场景中,成功识别出对所有可允许经典拓扑都表现出量子优势的通信任务,证实了拓扑无关量子优势的存在性和普遍性。

Conclusion: 本研究确立了拓扑独立性作为通信网络中一种真实且鲁棒的量子优势形式。拓扑无关量子优势表明量子协议可以在不依赖特定网络拓扑结构的条件下系统性地优于所有经典协议,即使经典方拥有任意通信资源分配和无限共享随机性。这一结果不仅深化了对量子通信本质优势的理解,还为量子网络的实际应用提供了更具鲁棒性的理论基础,特别是在网络配置未知或动态变化的场景中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Quantum communication can outperform classical communication in a variety of information-processing tasks, but such advantages are typically established for a fixed communication network. Here we introduce topology-independent quantum advantage, in which a quantum protocol outperforms every classical protocol subject to the same communication constraint, irrespective of how the classical communication resource is distributed among the parties or which communication topology is employed, even when the parties may share unlimited classical randomness. We first demonstrate this phenomenon through a simple three-party equality task: two senders receive ternary classical inputs, while an input-free receiver determines whether the inputs are equal. We then formulate a general framework for topology-independent communication and completely characterize the corresponding classical correlation polytope in the minimal three-party scenario. Its facet inequalities reveal several instances of topology-independent quantum advantage. Remarkably, one of these inequalities enables the semi-device-independent certification of a nonunitary quantum channel, while another certifies a sequential quantum network topology without any prior assumption about the network configuration. Finally, we extend the framework to four-party networks, characterize the relevant classical communication structures, and identify a communication task exhibiting quantum advantages against all admissible classical topologies. Our results establish topology independence as a genuine and robust form of quantum advantage in communication networks.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的无信号约束仅针对孤立结果概率,但无法确定设备在重复读出、网络组合或重用等扩展场景下是否能保持其可访问记录的联合分布规律。需要识别在将设备扩展到更丰富的记录和干预时,边际无信号条件之外还需要满足哪些附加条件。

Method: 采用条件记录准则(conditional-record criterion)和四个显式模型进行分析:(1)包裹柯西逆因果源模型,研究连续读出保护条件;(2)有限角带宽假设下的泄漏边界量化;(3)双源贝尔结点的校正矩阵分析,研究均匀角网格上的非负秩性质;(4)高斯控制器模型,分析分支质量依赖的消除及噪声与规律变化的权衡;(5)带共享经典噪声的固定单态模型,对比保留和刷新控制器的时间统计差异。通过有限资源阻碍定理和正有限控制器近似构造,并进行830万次独立模拟历史的数值验证。

Result: 对于包裹柯西逆因果源,两次条件独立连续读出的保护迫使角度无关响应,三次读出在无连续性假设下即充分。有限角带宽给出定量泄漏界,而单纯连续性无法保证均匀稳定性。指定双源贝尔结点处,所需校正矩阵在每个偶数均匀角网格上具有满非负秩。高斯控制器可消除分支质量依赖同时留下可区分的联合记录,导出精确的噪声与规律变化权衡关系。带共享经典噪声的固定单态再现了可变宽度孤立结果族,但保留和刷新控制器产生不同的时间统计。证明了有限资源阻碍定理并构造了正有限控制器近似。830万次独立模拟历史数值研究支持模型特定预测。

Conclusion: 研究识别了在将设备扩展到更丰富的记录和干预时,必须伴随边际无信号条件的附加约束。结果表明:连续读出保护、有限角带宽、校正矩阵的非负秩性质、噪声与规律变化权衡、以及控制器刷新策略的选择,都对保持联合记录分布至关重要。这些条件共同构成了超越孤立结果概率无信号约束的扩展理论框架,为量子测量设备的重复使用和网络组合提供了理论依据。

Abstract: No-signalling constraints on isolated outcome probabilities do not determine whether an apparatus preserves the joint law of its accessible records under repeated readout, network composition, or reuse. We study these extensions through a conditional-record criterion and four explicit models. For a wrapped-Cauchy retrocausal source, protection under two conditionally independent continuous readouts forces an angle-independent response; three readouts suffice without continuity. A finite angular-bandwidth assumption gives a quantitative leakage bound, whereas continuity alone gives no uniform stability. At a specified two-source Bell junction, the required correction matrix has full nonnegative rank on every even uniform angular grid. A Gaussian controller can cancel branch-mass dependence while leaving a distinguishable joint record, for which we derive exact noise and law-change tradeoffs. Finally, a fixed singlet with shared classical noise reproduces the variable-width isolated outcome family, but retained and refreshed controllers generate different temporal statistics. We prove a finite-resource obstruction and construct positive finite-controller approximations. Numerical studies comprising 8.3 million independent simulated histories support the model-specific predictions. Together, the results identify conditions that must accompany marginal no-signalling when extending an apparatus to richer records and interventions.

</details>

Method: 采用基于张量网络的分层运动方程(HEOM)方法的量子动力学框架,考虑具有多个相关跃迁频率和一般谐振环境的反应坐标。扩展了有效谱密度的简洁表述,并将马尔可夫浴模式压缩为系统级跳跃算符。通过计算振动态之间的态-态概率流,分解反应网络中的概率流分布,分析腔诱导的局部跃迁变化如何在多步反应网络中传播。

Abstract: Recent experiments suggest that strong light-matter coupling between molecules and cavity modes can significantly modify chemical reaction rates and yields. Despite multiple proposed mechanisms, a clear microscopic understanding of how vibrational strong coupling alters reaction kinetics remains incomplete. Here, we employ a general quantum-dynamical framework for analyzing competing reaction pathways in polaritonic chemistry by considering reaction coordinates with multiple relevant transition frequencies and general harmonic environments. We extend on a tensor-network formulation of the hierarchical equations of motion (HEOM) method to compute probability currents between vibrational states, leveraging a concise formulation for effective spectral densities and a controlled compression of Markovian bath modes into system-level jump operators. This allows us to decompose the resulting state-to-state probability currents, revealing how cavity-induced changes in individual transitions propagate through multistep reaction networks. We find that these local modifications can alter kinetic bottlenecks and selectively redistribute reaction flux between competing pathways, changing branching ratios under irreversible conditions without substantially affecting the equilibrium yield in the reversible regime. Finally, for coupled reaction-coordinate, spectator-mode, and cavity environments, we find structured, asymmetric rate profiles arising from interference between hybridized decay channels. Together, these results provide a mechanistic framework for understanding pathway-specific chemical reactivity under vibrational strong coupling.

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一种可扩展的外延约瑟夫森结制备工艺，成功减少了非晶介电材料的使用并降低了制备复杂度。制备的NbN/AlN/NbN结具有优异的电学性能和宽范围的临界电流密度可调性，为开发高相干氮化物基超导量子比特提供了有前景的技术平台。

Abstract: Epitaxial NbN/AlN/NbN Josephson junctions are promising alternatives to conventional Al/AlO$_x$/Al junctions for superconducting qubits due to their crystalline tunnel barriers and the high transition temperature of NbN. However, their integration into quantum circuits typically requires complex fabrication processes involving amorphous interlayer dielectrics, which can degrade qubit coherence times. To address these issues, we developed sidewall spacer passivated epitaxial NbN/AlN/NbN junctions using a fabrication process that reduces both the volume of amorphous dielectric material and fabrication complexity. The fabricated junctions exhibited a large gap voltage of approximately 5.6 mV and low subgap leakage currents. By varying the thickness of the AlN tunnel barrier, high-quality junctions were fabricated over a wide range of critical current densities ${\it J}_c$, from 0.3 to 10$^4$ A/cm$^2$. In addition, the comparison of the current-voltage characteristics before and after buffered hydrofluoric acid treatment indicated that the wet etching process did not degrade the electrical characteristics of the junctions. These results establish a scalable fabrication process for epitaxial Josephson junctions and provide a promising platform for high-coherence nitride-based superconducting qubits.

Abstract: We report a demonstration of a self-shunted superconducting flux qubit (SSFQ) as an ultracompact, strongly anharmonic, and long-lifetime qubit by using the full epitaxially grown NbN/AlN/NbN Josephson junctions. The NbN-junction-based SSFQ achieved a high anharmonicity of 797 MHz as well as drastically reduced footprint of the qubit compared to capacitively shunted (C-shunt) flux qubits with a large shunt capacitor. The lifetime of the SSFQ reached 13 - 25 $μ$s, which is comparable to or relatively higher than that of NbN-based C-shunt flux qubits and Al-based flux qubits with more than 10 times smaller junctions. This indicates that the large epitaxial NbN-based junction can be adopted for SSFQ as well as merged-element transmon with the relatively large junctions. The results presented in this work will offer a new choice of the scalable qubits as well as the material platform toward the realization of large-scale superconducting quantum computers.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 1. 针对有限维量子信道，开发基于纠缠生成势的理论框架，建立精确的摊销定理（amortization theorem），界定消息信息增量与实验室间剩余纠缠的总和。2. 利用Winter和Yang研究的混合凸顶表达式（mixed-convex-roof expression）证明经典反馈容量界。3. 针对信道容量：分析标记的无噪声与纠缠破缺信道混合、改进去极化信道逆定理、精确评估振幅阻尼信道的新界。4. 针对状态类比：推导单向经典通信下可获得净共享随机性的有限码长上界，使用振幅阻尼Choi态和广义振幅阻尼Choi态作为基准测试。5. 设计两轮弱测量协议，在第一次解码时保留量子边信息并在反向提取额外随机性，以区分单向与双向随机性蒸馏。

Abstract: We develop entanglement-of-formation potentials for feedback-assisted classical communication and one-way randomness distillation. For a finite-dimensional quantum channel without initial shared entanglement, an exact amortization theorem bounds the increase of message information plus entanglement remaining across the communicating laboratories. This proves a classical-feedback capacity bound using a mixed-convex-roof expression previously studied by Winter and Yang in the context of potential capacities. We determine the capacity of flagged mixtures of noiseless and entanglement-breaking channels, improve a published depolarizing-channel converse, and evaluate the new bound exactly for the amplitude damping channel. A state analogue gives a finite-blocklength upper bound on the net shared randomness obtainable with one-way classical communication. Amplitude-damping Choi states provide exact benchmarks, while a full-rank generalized amplitude-damping Choi state demonstrates a useful improvement where we do not evaluate the operational rate exactly. We also distinguish one-way from two-way randomness distillation: a two-round weak-measurement protocol strictly exceeds the exact one-way rate of every nontrivial qubit isotropic state. The protocol preserves quantum side information during its first decoding and extracts additional randomness in the reverse direction. This shows why the one-way state potential is not a general two-way converse. Together, these results establish a common entanglement-based approach to sharper communication and randomness bounds, while revealing how retained quantum correlations enable two-way distillation to surpass one-way limits.

</details>


### [97] [Quantum codes in the Lee metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04834)
*Jinkang Guo,Yiqiu Han,Aranya Chakraborty,Shubham P. Jain,Tianhao Liu,Victor V. Albert,Andrew Lucas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对离散小位移噪声提出了量子编码框架，将qudit上的错误建模为低权重的X和Z型Pauli位移。通过引入Lee度量扩展经典Lee度量编码理论到量子稳定子码，并证明了量子CSS Lee-LDPC码的Lee距离存在O(n)上界，同时发现某些经典螺旋重复码在局部动力学下具有指数级记忆时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子纠错码主要基于Hamming度量设计，但在核自旋噪声等实际物理系统中，错误更符合小位移特征（类似连续变量系统中的小相空间位移）。Lee度量能够更好地刻画这种低权重Pauli位移错误结构，因此需要发展基于Lee度量的量子编码理论，以更高效地利用物理量子比特资源并探索高维qudit系统的编码潜力。

Method: 1) 针对任意q值建立Z_q上稳定子码的Lee度量形式化理论，包括联合度量和分离度量；2) 分析Lee权重在Clifford门下的传播规律；3) 通过Gray映射将Z_4上的码转化为qubit码，寻找具有双倍横向非qubit-Clifford门的码；4) 对量子CSS Lee-LDPC码进行理论分析，证明Lee距离上界；5) 研究经典q元螺旋重复码在局部Metropolis动力学下的行为；6) 构造这些螺旋重复码的超图乘积以获得局部二维量子码。

Result: 1) 对于qubit系统，联合Lee度量将Y错误计数两次，可以用更少的物理量子比特检测和纠正X和Z错误；2) 证明了n个qudit上的量子CSS Lee-LDPC码的Lee距离不能超过O(n)（在q上一致），揭示了利用大q值qudit的大内部Hilbert空间构造高Lee距离码存在意外障碍；3) 发现经典q元螺旋重复码在足够大的q值（随系统长度增长）下，在固定温度的局部Metropolis动力学中具有指数级长的记忆时间；4) 这些螺旋重复码的超图乘积提供了局部二维量子码，对Z错误具有自纠正性。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了基于Lee度量的量子稳定子码理论框架，更适合描述核自旋噪声等小位移错误模型。关键发现包括：Lee度量下的码可以用更少资源实现纠错，但量子CSS Lee-LDPC码的Lee距离存在O(n)基本限制，这限制了高维qudit系统的编码优势。经典螺旋重复码展现出有趣的有限温度自发对称性破缺现象，其量子版本对Z错误具有自纠正性，但X错误的自纠正性仍是开放问题，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: We introduce a quantum coding framework for discrete small-shift noise, in which errors on qudits are modeled as low-weight $X$- and $Z$-type Pauli shifts, analogous to small phase-space displacements in continuous-variable systems. This structure is approximately respected by nuclear-spin noise and captured by the Lee metric, motivating a quantum extension of classical Lee-metric coding theory. We develop such a formalism for stabilizer codes over $\mathbb{Z}_q$ for arbitrary $q$, using joint and separate Lee metrics for the $X$- and $Z$-components of errors. For qubits, the joint metric counts $Y$ errors twice and can yield codes that detect and correct $X$ and $Z$ errors with fewer physical qubits than codes designed for the conventional Hamming metric. We discuss Lee-weight spreading under Clifford gates and qubitize codes over $\mathbb{Z}_4$ via the Gray map, finding codes with two-fold transversal non-qubit-Clifford gates. For quantum CSS Lee-LDPC codes on $n$ qudits, we prove that the Lee distance cannot exceed $O(n)$, uniformly in $q$, demonstrating an unexpected obstruction to using the large internal Hilbert space of a large-$q$ qudit to make high-Lee-distance codes. Under local Metropolis dynamics, certain classical $q$-ary ``helical repetition codes'' have exponentially long memory times at fixed temperature for sufficiently large $q$ (that grows with system length), which can be understood as spontaneous symmetry breaking at finite temperature, even in local one-dimensional models. Hypergraph products of these helical repetition codes provide local two-dimensional quantum codes that inherit self-correction for $Z$ errors, but self-correction for $X$ errors remains an open question.

</details>


### [98] [Long-lived Coherent Phonons Reveal Competing Thermal and Many-Body Dynamics in $α$-In$_{2}$Se$_{3}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04863)
*Xuanchao Zhang,Dehao Yuan,Junhua Zhou,Vandana Tiwari,Fulu Zheng,Ajay Jha,Hong-Guang Duan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究使用超快瞬态光栅光谱技术研究了铁电α-In₂Se₃在光激发后的量子相干性演化，揭示了极性范德华半导体如何从相干晶格运动向非相干多体态转变的物理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低维固体中，电子、结构和多体能量尺度强耦合，光激发后量子相干性的命运是非平衡凝聚态物理的核心问题。需要独立探测晶格相干性、热涨落和载流子密度依赖的相互作用，以理解极性范德华半导体的超快动力学行为。

Method: 采用约5飞秒激光脉搏的宽带瞬态光栅光谱技术，在10-300 K温度范围内对铁电α-In₂Se₃进行测量；通过改变激发能量研究载流子密度效应；结合第一性原理计算确定声子模式归属；利用量子动力学模拟重现瞬态光栅振荡行为。

Result: 光激发后产生持续数皮秒的~104 cm⁻¹主导模式振荡；该模式频率在10-300 K范围内几乎不变，但相干性逐渐被抑制，表明晶格坐标与热激活退相位过程可区分；增加光激发强度出现明显交叉行为，逐渐改变并抑制相干响应；第一性原理计算将主振荡归属为101.47 cm⁻¹的Γ点光学声子（涉及In-Se集体位移）；量子动力学模拟成功再现主要瞬态光栅振荡特征。

Conclusion: 研究确立了α-In₂Se₃作为独立探测晶格相干性、热涨落和载流子密度依赖相互作用的模型体系，揭示了极性范德华半导体在超快激发后如何从相干晶格运动演化为非相干多体态，为理解低维材料中量子相干性与多体效应的竞争提供了重要实验和理论依据。

Abstract: The fate of quantum coherence following photoexcitation is a central problem in nonequilibrium condensed-matter physics, especially in low-dimensional solids where electronic, structural and many-body energy scales are strongly coupled. Here, we investigate this interplay in ferroelectric $α$-In$_{2}$Se$_{3}$ using broadband transient-grating spectroscopy with $\sim$5 fs laser pulses. Photoexcitation launches pronounced oscillations dominated by a $\sim$104 cm$^{-1}$ mode that persists for several picoseconds. Measurements from 10 to 300 K show that its frequency remains nearly unchanged while its coherence is progressively suppressed, distinguishing the lattice coordinate from thermally activated dephasing. Varying excitation energy reveals a distinct crossover in which increasing photoexcitation progressively modifies and damps the coherent response. First-principles calculations assign the dominant oscillation to a 101.47 cm$^{-1}$ $Γ$-point optical phonon involving collective In-Se displacement, while quantum-dynamical simulations reproduce the main transient-grating oscillations. These results establish $α$-In$_{2}$Se$_{3}$ as a model system for independently probing lattice coherence, thermal fluctuations and carrier-density-dependent interactions, revealing how a polar van der Waals semiconductor evolves from coherent lattice motion toward an incoherent many-body state after ultrafast excitation.

</details>


### [99] [Custom Penalty QAOA via Walsh-Hadamard Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04908)
*Xin Wei LEE,Siong Thye GOH,Hoong Chuin LAU*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了Walsh自定义惩罚QAOA (WCP-QAOA)框架,通过Walsh-Hadamard变换将非线性惩罚函数转化为Pauli-Z串的加权和,实现了无松弛变量的组合优化约束处理,在不增加量子比特数的前提下有效解决了量子近似优化算法中的不等式约束问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统组合优化中的不等式约束处理需要引入松弛变量和二次惩罚项,这会增加量子比特数量并扭曲优化景观。虽然无松弛变量方法直接对约束应用非线性自定义惩罚函数,但这类函数难以在量子近似优化算法(QAOA)中实现。因此需要找到一种方法,既能使用自定义惩罚函数,又能在QAOA框架下高效实现,同时避免增加量子比特开销。

Method: 提出WCP-QAOA框架:利用Walsh-Hadamard变换(WHT)将应用于线性约束的任意惩罚函数精确转换为Pauli-Z串的加权和。在Walsh度数k处截断展开式,仅需O(n^k)个Pauli项且不增加量子比特数。该截断是惩罚函数在L²范数下的最优k度逼近。针对指数惩罚函数,推导出WHT的闭式乘积形式和Walsh系数的闭式表达式。开发了次指数时间算法来计算Walsh系数,使得对于固定k,度数k的哈密顿量可在多项式时间内获得。提出了缩短精确谱计算的结构化快捷方法。

Result: 理论上证明了WHT能够移除QAOA实现自定义惩罚函数的障碍,截断后的哈密顿量在特定条件下仍能保证所有可行解排序低于所有不可行解。对于指数惩罚函数获得了Walsh系数的闭式解。实验上在6量子比特背包问题上测试了WCP-QAOA的有效性。算法复杂度方面,固定度数k时计算复杂度为多项式时间,大幅优于朴素方法的指数时间复杂度。

Conclusion: WCP-QAOA框架通过Walsh-Hadamard变换成功解决了在量子近似优化算法中实现自定义非线性惩罚函数的技术难题,在不增加量子比特数的情况下实现了高效的约束处理。该方法提供了理论保证的最优逼近性质,并通过闭式表达式和高效算法降低了计算复杂度。这为量子计算在约束组合优化问题上的应用提供了新的实用途径,特别是在量子比特资源受限的近期量子设备上具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Inequality constraints in combinatorial optimization are conventionally handled by introducing slack variables and quadratic penalties, which increase the qubit count and distort the optimization landscape. Slack-free formulations instead apply a nonlinear custom penalty function directly to the constraint, which are difficult to be implemented in quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA). We show that the Walsh--Hadamard transform (WHT) removes this obstruction for any penalty function: a penalty applied to a linear constraint is exactly a weighted sum of Pauli-$Z$ strings. We propose this framework as the Walsh custom penalty QAOA (WCP-QAOA). Truncating the expansion at Walsh degree $k$ costs $\mathcal{O}(n^k)$ Pauli terms and leaves the qubit count untouched. The truncation is the optimal degree-$k$ approximation of the penalty in $L^2$, and we give a condition on the discarded Walsh coefficients under which the truncated Hamiltonian still ranks every feasible solution below every infeasible one. Since evaluating a Walsh coefficient from its definition requires all $2^n$ penalty values, we give an algorithm that returns any coefficient in sub-exponential time, so that the degree-$k$ Hamiltonian is obtained in time polynomial in $n$ for fixed $k$. We show that for the exponential penalty, there exists a closed product form of WHT, and hence a closed form expression to calculate the Walsh coefficients. We identify structural shortcuts that shorten the exact spectrum. We also tested WCP-QAOA in solving a 6-qubit knapsack problem.

</details>


### [100] [Pairwise detection, chiral conversion: Resource theory of quantum multistates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04930)
*Yuan-Hong Tao,Yu-Xin Wu,Bing Xie,Lin Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了量子多态在保交换性操作下的精确资源转换理论。对于量子比特，成对Bargmann不变量决定唯一候选信道的奇异值，而三阶Bargmann相位决定其取向。完全正性条件给出精确转换的充要判据，揭示了手性敏感壳层，其中具有相同成对几何的目标态仅因三次不变量符号差异而表现出不同的可转换性，实现了检测阶数k=2与转换阶数k=3的分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多态的基独立相干性由其组成态是否对易决定，对于量子比特可通过成对态重叠检测，但其确定性转换的支配规律一直是开放问题。现有理论缺乏对保交换性操作下量子多态资源转换的完整刻画，特别是转换判据与几何不变量之间的精确关系尚不清楚。

Method: 1) 研究保交换性操作(CPOs)下量子多态的资源转换理论框架；2) 对于量子比特系统，利用成对Bargmann不变量确定候选信道的奇异值，用三阶Bargmann相位确定其取向；3) 通过完全正性条件推导精确转换的充要判据；4) 分析手性敏感壳层的结构和性质；5) 对高维系统(d≥3)研究转换的刚性结构，识别各向同性去极化信道和转置去极化信道的作用。

Result: 对于量子比特多态(n≥3)：实现了检测阶数k_detect=2与转换阶数k_convert=3的阶数分离；识别出手性敏感壳层C_chir，占据奇异值单纯形的恰好三分之一；镜像反射按体积阻碍了三分之二的保取向变换。对于高维系统(d≥3)：结构呈现刚性，所有资源型转换仅由各向同性去极化或转置去极化信道实现；可转换性由阶数至多为d²的中心化Bargmann不变量决定。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了量子多态在保交换性操作下的确定性转换问题。揭示了量子比特系统中手性在资源转换中的本质作用：具有相同成对几何特征的态可仅因三次不变量符号不同而表现出不同的可转换性。这一发现确立了从检测到转换的阶数跃升（从2阶到3阶），表明量子相干资源的转换需要比检测更高阶的不变量信息。高维系统的刚性结构则表明转换机制随维度呈现质的变化，为量子资源理论提供了精确的数学刻画。

Abstract: Basis-independent coherence of a quantum multistate is fixed by whether its constituent states commute, and for qubits it is detected by pairwise state overlaps alone. What governs its deterministic transformation has been open. We give the exact resource-conversion theory of quantum multistates under common commutativity-preserving operations (CPOs). For qubits, pairwise Bargmann invariants determine the singular values of the unique candidate channel, while a single third-order Bargmann phase determines its orientation. Complete positivity then yields a necessary and sufficient criterion for exact conversion. The criterion identifies a chirality-sensitive shell $\mathcal C_{\rm chir}$, within which targets with identical pairwise geometry can differ in convertibility solely by the sign of their cubic invariant. This yields an order separation, $k_{\rm detect}=2<3=k_{\rm convert}$, for qubit multistates of every size $n\geqslant3$. The chirality-sensitive shell occupies exactly one third of the singular-value simplex, while mirror reflection obstructs two thirds of the admissible orientation-preserving transformations by volume. For $d\geqslant3$, by contrast, the structure is rigid: every resourceful conversion is realized by either an isotropic depolarizing or a transpose-depolarizing channel. Convertibility is then decided by centered Bargmann invariants of degree at most $d^2$.

</details>


### [101] [Gap Amplification for Local Hamiltonians with Combinatorial Soundness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04952)
*Mitali Bafna,Quynh T. Nguyen,Tina Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个可行的量子局部性保持间隙放大模板，通过容错计算框架证明了该方法能够放大局部哈密顿量的组合间隙，为将经典PCP定理的核心技术引入量子环境提供了新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子PCP猜想是量子复杂性理论中的重要未解问题。由于量子不可克隆定理，经典PCP定理证明中的关键技术（如局部性保持间隙放大和字母表约简）难以直接推广到量子情形。局部性保持间隙放大要求在不增加哈密顿量局部性的前提下扩大承诺间隙，这对证明经典PCP定理至关重要，但在量子环境中尚无明确的对应方法。

Method: 本文提出了一个基于容错计算的量子间隙放大新框架。该方法结合了三个关键技术：(1) Bafna-Minzer-Vyas基于高维扩展图的经典PCP构造思想；(2) Anshu-Breuckmann-Nguyen的电路到哈密顿量构造方法；(3) 量子编码理论的最新进展。通过这些技术的融合，构建了首个量子局部性保持间隙放大的可行模板。

Result: 该研究证明了所提出的方法能够成功放大局部哈密顿量的组合间隙，同时保持局部性不变（仅适度增加局部量子位维度）。这是首次在量子环境中实现局部性保持的间隙放大，为量子PCP猜想的研究提供了新的技术工具。

Conclusion: 本工作为量子局部性保持间隙放大提供了首个可行方案，成功将经典PCP定理证明中的核心技术引入量子设置。通过容错计算框架的创新应用，该研究为解决量子PCP猜想开辟了新的研究路径，并展示了如何克服量子不可克隆带来的根本性障碍。

Abstract: The quantum PCP conjecture is one of the major open problems in quantum complexity theory. It has resisted attack in part because many primitives used in the proof of the classical PCP theorem, such as locality-preserving gap amplification and alphabet reduction, have no obvious quantum analogues due to quantum no-cloning. Locality-preserving gap amplification is a procedure that takes as input a local Hamiltonian problem instance and produces a new instance with a larger promise gap, without increasing the locality of the Hamiltonian, and instead moderately increasing its local qudit dimension. Obtaining this kind of control over the locality during gap amplification is critical to the success of many known strategies for proving the classical PCP theorem. In this work, we put forth the first known viable template for quantum locality-preserving gap amplification, and we prove that our procedure amplifies the combinatorial gap of local Hamiltonians. Our work introduces a new framework for reasoning about quantum gap amplification in terms of fault-tolerant computation, and illuminates a route toward importing one of the central ingredients in classical PCPs into the quantum setting. In particular, we build upon ideas from the recent classical PCP of Bafna--Minzer--Vyas based on high-dimensional expanders, and the circuit-to-Hamiltonian construction of Anshu--Breuckmann--Nguyen, in addition to several recent advances in quantum coding theory.

</details>


### [102] [Improved Upper and Lower Bounds for Quantum Convex-Body Volume Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04987)
*Ruizhe Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对高维凸体体积估计问题,提出了一种改进的量子算法,将查询复杂度降低至 $\widetilde O(d^{5/2}+d^{3/2}/\varepsilon)$,优于之前的量子算法和经典算法,并将量子查询下界从 $Ω(\sqrt d)$ 提升至 $Ω(d)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维凸体体积估计是理论计算机科学中的基本问题。现有的量子算法查询复杂度为 $\widetilde O(d^3+d^{9/4}/\varepsilon)$,而经典算法的最优上界为 $\widetilde O(d^{7/2}+d^3/\varepsilon^2)$。本研究旨在进一步降低量子算法的查询复杂度,扩大相对于经典算法的量子加速优势,同时改进量子查询下界。

Method: 算法结合了两个新技术组件:(1) 基于均匀经典热启动混合界的量子行走的输入依赖有效谱间隙分析;(2) 基于经典桥采样的模拟退火中归一化比率乘积的单一量子估计器。这些技术共同实现了量子查询复杂度的改进。

Result: 提出的量子算法将查询复杂度降低至 $\widetilde O(d^{5/2}+d^{3/2}/\varepsilon)$,优于先前的量子上界 $\widetilde O(d^3+d^{9/4}/\varepsilon)$(Chakrabarti et al., ACM TQC 2023 和 Cornelissen-Hamoudi, SODA 2023),并相对于当前最优经典上界 $\widetilde O(d^{7/2}+d^3/\varepsilon^2)$(Jia et al., JACM 2026)实现了更大的量子加速。同时,将常数相对误差下的量子查询下界从 $Ω(\sqrt d)$ 提升至 $Ω(d)$。

Conclusion: 本文显著改进了高维凸体体积估计的量子算法效率,在查询复杂度上取得了实质性突破,相对于经典算法展现出更强的量子优势。新提出的量子行走谱间隙分析和量子估计器技术为解决该类问题提供了新的理论工具。更紧的量子下界结果也进一步刻画了该问题的量子复杂性边界。

Abstract: Estimating the volume of a high-dimensional convex body is a fundamental problem in theoretical computer science. Given a quantum membership oracle for a convex body $K\subset\mathbb{R}^d$, we show that estimating $\mathrm{vol}(K)$ to relative error $\varepsilon$ takes $\widetilde O(d^{5/2}+d^{3/2}/\varepsilon)$ queries. This improves the previous quantum upper bound $\widetilde O(d^3+d^{9/4}/\varepsilon)$ of Chakrabarti et al. (ACM TQC 2023) and Cornelissen--Hamoudi (SODA 2023), and achieves a larger quantum speedup over the currently best classical upper bound $\widetilde O(d^{7/2}+d^3/\varepsilon^2)$ of Jia et al. (JACM 2026). Our quantum algorithm combines two new ingredients: an input-dependent effective spectral-gap analysis of quantum walks based on uniform classical warm-start mixing bounds, and a single quantum estimator for products of normalizer ratios in simulated annealing based on classical bridge sampling. We also prove an $Ω(d)$ quantum query lower bound for constant relative error, improving the previous $Ω(\sqrt d)$ lower bound.

</details>


### [103] [Blind Remote Implementation of Arbitrary Two-Qubit Unitary Operation via Pre-Shared Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04995)
*Plaban Saha,Binayak S. Choudhury*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用预共享纠缠态远程实现任意两量子比特幺正操作的协议，该协议基于单量子比特的欧拉分解和两量子比特的嘉当分解，使得Alice可以使用校准的黑盒设备对Bob持有的未知量子态实施操作，而无需知晓操作参数或量子态的具体信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算中需要远程实现任意幺正操作，但传统方法要求操作者知道幺正操作的经典描述参数。为了在保护量子态隐私和操作参数保密的前提下实现远程量子操作，需要设计一种协议，使得Alice能够使用黑盒设备对Bob的未知量子态进行操作，同时Bob的量子态无需离开其实验室，且Alice和Bob都不需要知道幺正操作的具体参数。

Method: 该协议基于单量子比特幺正操作的欧拉分解和两量子比特幺正操作的嘉当分解。首先，利用预共享的贝尔态构建远程实现单量子比特操作U = R_z(γ)R_x(β)R_z(α)的协议；然后，使用单个预共享贝尔态构建两量子比特操作R_zz(θ)的协议，并通过局域幺正变换将其扩展至R_xx(θ)和R_yy(θ)；最后，将这些基本协议组合起来实现任意两量子比特幺正操作。第三方Charlie负责确定所需的分解形式，并在协议开始前向Alice提供相应的黑盒设备。

Result: 成功构建了远程实现任意两量子比特幺正操作的完整协议。该协议实现了以下功能：(1)Alice使用校准的黑盒设备完成操作而无需知道操作参数；(2)Bob的未知量子态始终保持在其实验室内；(3)通过组合单量子比特旋转操作(R_z、R_x)和两量子比特旋转操作(R_zz、R_xx、R_yy)的基本协议，可以实现任意两量子比特幺正操作；(4)Alice和Bob均不需要幺正操作的经典描述。

Conclusion: 本文提出的协议成功解决了在预共享纠缠态条件下远程实现任意两量子比特幺正操作的问题。通过引入第三方Charlie提供黑盒设备，协议实现了操作参数对Alice和Bob的保密性，同时保证了Bob量子态的本地性。该协议基于量子操作的分解理论，通过组合基本的单量子比特和两量子比特旋转操作，为分布式量子计算和量子通信提供了一种新的实现方案，在量子隐私保护和安全量子计算方面具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: We propose a protocol for the remote implementation of an arbitrary two-qubit unitary operation using pre-shared entanglement. In our protocol, Bob possesses an unknown two-qubit state, while Alice implements the required operations using calibrated black-box devices. Alice does not need to know the parameters of these operations, and Bob's unknown state never leaves his laboratory. The protocol is based on the Euler decomposition of single-qubit unitary operations and the Cartan decomposition of two-qubit unitary operations. First, we construct protocols for the remote implementation of the single-qubit operation $U = R_z(γ)R_x(β)R_z(α)$ using pre-shared Bell states. We then construct a protocol for the two-qubit operation $R_{zz}(θ)$ using a single pre-shared Bell state and extend it to $R_{xx}(θ)$ and $R_{yy}(θ)$ through local unitary transformations. These elementary protocols are finally combined to implement an arbitrary two-qubit unitary operation. A third party, Charlie, determines the required decomposition and provides Alice with the corresponding black-box devices before the protocol begins. Thus, neither Alice nor Bob requires the classical description of the unitary operation.

</details>


### [104] [The power of time reversal in Hamiltonian property testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04996)
*Richard R. Allen,Matthias C. Caro,Yanlin Chen,Tom Gur,Angus Lowe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了时间反演是量子算法在哈密顿量性质测试中实现海森堡极限精度所必需的资源，建立了从平均情况到最坏情况的演化时间下界，并提出了一种新的傅里叶分析方法用于连续时间量子下界证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在传感和学习任务中，量子算法可以实现海森堡极限精度O(1/ε)，但在哈密顿量性质测试中，已知最佳算法需要O(1/ε²)的总演化时间，除非能够在哈密顿量下实现时间反演。本研究旨在严格证明时间反演对于弥合这一精度差距的必要性，并建立时间反演作为量子测量精度和算法鲁棒性资源的理论基础。

Method: 提出了一种新的基于傅里叶分析的连续时间量子下界证明方法，该方法对时间反演敏感且适用范围超越哈密顿量性质测试。针对从平均情况到最坏情况的连续误差度量族，建立了哈密顿量认证和局域性测试的演化时间下界。同时证明了在有无时间反演两种情况下哈密顿量认证的匹配上界。将该方法扩展应用于振幅放大与估计、分数查询以及非结构化搜索问题。

Result: 证明了在哈密顿量认证和局域性测试中，没有时间反演时必须使用O(1/ε²)演化时间，而有时间反演时可达到O(1/ε)。为所有情况的哈密顿量认证建立了匹配的上下界，显示时间反演的优势持续存在。改进了Tang和Wright关于振幅放大与估计需要逆运算的下界证明，并扩展到分数查询。证明了在非结构化搜索中，预言机相位的不确定性会破坏量子加速，即使预言机是固定且相干的。

Conclusion: 本研究严格确立了时间反演作为支配量子测量精度和量子算法鲁棒性的关键资源。证明了时间反演对于在哈密顿量性质测试中实现海森堡极限是必需的，没有时间反演能力的算法在精度上存在本质性的二次方差距。提出的傅里叶分析框架为连续时间量子算法的下界证明提供了新工具，具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms enable Heisenberg-limited scaling across many tasks in sensing and learning, achieving precision $\varepsilon$ using $O(1/\varepsilon)$ total evolution time. However, in Hamiltonian property testing, the best known algorithms have $O(1/\varepsilon^2)$ total evolution time, unless it is possible to $\textit{reverse}$ the flow of time under the Hamiltonian. In this work, we prove that time reversal is necessary to close this gap. We establish evolution time lower bounds for Hamiltonian certification and locality testing, for a continuous family of error metrics ranging from average- to worst-case. We also prove matching upper bounds for Hamiltonian certification in all cases, both with and without time reversal, showing that the advantage from time reversal persists. Our results follow from a new Fourier-analytic approach to quantum lower bounds in continuous time, which is sensitive to time reversal and applicable beyond Hamiltonian property testing. This gives an alternative proof that inverses are required for amplitude amplification and estimation, improving the lower bounds of Tang and Wright and extending to fractional queries. We also show that, for unstructured search, uncertainty in the phase of the oracle destroys the quantum speedup without inverse queries, even when the oracle is fixed and coherent. Our work rigorously establishes time reversal as a resource governing both the precision of quantum measurement and the robustness of quantum algorithms.

</details>

Method: 构建了由两个稀释制冷机(节点A和B)通过30米长低温量子链路连接的原始双节点超导电路QED网络。采用双向光子传输协议:节点A的通信量子比特发射微波光子,该光子在远程节点B反射时根据节点B量子比特的状态获得条件相位。通过调节通信光子频率来调控反射过程中获得的条件相位,从而实现任意相位的受控Z(CZ)门。利用该纠缠操作实现从节点A对节点B量子比特的单次测量读出。

Result: 成功在空间分离的超导量子节点间实现了纠缠门操作,验证了任意相位CZ门的可调性和有效性。实现了从节点A对节点B的远程单次测量读出功能。关键特性是该方案无需主动节点与远程节点之间的精确同步,远程读出可以在远程节点无任何主动参与的情况下完成,显著降低了协议复杂度。

Conclusion: 本工作在超导电路平台上成功演示了跨物理分离节点的纠缠门操作和远程量子比特读出,为超导量子通信工具箱增添了新的协议。该方案的免同步特性使其在实际分布式量子计算中更具实用性,为实现基本分布式量子计算操作提供了可行途径,推动了量子网络和分布式量子计算的发展。

Abstract: Establishing a complete set of gates across spatially separated quantum nodes is fundamental for distributed quantum computation and communication networks. One of the approaches to do so consists of employing a superconducting device as a universal quantum node that is capable of storing and processing quantum information, and traveling microwave photons as means of communication. In this work, we realize an entangling gate across a primitive two-node superconducting circuit QED network consisting of two dilution cryostats, A and B, connected via a 30-m-long cryogenic quantum link. We perform a bidirectional photon transfer protocol, where a microwave photon emitted by the communication qubit at A acquires a conditional phase based on the state of qubit B. We vary the communication photon frequency, hence tuning the conditional phase acquired during the reflection off the remote node, effectively implementing an arbitrary-phase CZ gate. We use this entangling operation to perform single-shot readout of the qubit in node B from node A. An interesting feature of our scheme is that it does not require precise synchronization of the active node with the remote node; in particular, remote readout can be performed without any active participation by the remote node. These protocols add to the quantum communication toolbox with superconducting circuits and provide a way of realizing basic distributed quantum computing operations.

</details>

Result: 改进后的方法能够高效模拟包含数百个状态的量子系统,相比传统PT-MPO方法可处理的系统维度(几十个状态)有了数量级的提升。该方法在固态腔量子电动力学多模系统以及通过传感器系统耦合计算频率滤波关联函数等应用中表现出显著优势。

Conclusion: 通过利用部分耦合特性和引入泰勒展开技术,成功将PT-MPO方法的计算复杂度降低到O(d^3),极大地扩展了该方法的适用范围,使其能够处理数百个状态的大规模开放量子系统,为固态腔QED和频率滤波关联函数计算等领域提供了强大的数值工具。

Abstract: Process tensors in matrix product operator representation (PT-MPOs) are a powerful tool to calculate the temporal evolution and multi-time correlations of open quantum systems. While much progress has been made in making the calculation of process tensors for larger system dimensions possible, the numerical cost of the subsequent propagation of the system typically scales as $\mathcal{O}(d^6)$, which renders PT-MPO methods prohibitively expensive for system dimensions above several dozens. Here, we show how PT-MPO methods can be used efficiently when only some of the system states couple to the environment, and how combination with a Taylor expansion method allows for a reduction of the numerical complexity down to $\mathcal{O}(d^3)$. We show that these methods enable efficient simulations of systems with several hundreds of states. This is particularly relevant for solid-state cavity-QED systems with multiple modes, or when calculating frequency-filtered correlation functions by coupling sensor systems to the system of interest.

</details>

*Tuan-Vu Truong,Vu-Quoc-Minh Nguyen,Hoang-Long Nguyen,Thanh-Tien Nguyen*

TL;DR: 本文研究了正方晶格和双层结构上的横场轴向次近邻伊辛(ANNNI)模型。在经典多相点处，通过横场微扰理论(至8阶)研究量子涨落对相结构的选择作用，发现量子效应按Fisher-Selke序列依次稳定不同的调制相，并用随机级数展开量子蒙特卡罗方法验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典ANNNI模型在多相点κ=1/2处存在大量简并基态(由直畴壁分隔的构型)。需要研究横向量子场如何打破这种简并性，选择出特定的有序相结构，以及第二层对相稳定性的影响。这对理解量子涨落在竞争相互作用系统中的作用具有重要意义。

Method: 1) 在经典多相点κ=J₂/J₀=1/2处,对每个畴壁构型分别进行横场g=Γ/J₀的微扰理论展开,计算至g⁸阶; 2) 分析不同阶次上量子涨落对各调制相(⟨3⟩、⟨23⟩、⟨223⟩、⟨2223⟩等)能量的贡献; 3) 使用随机级数展开(SSE)量子蒙特卡罗方法,通过热力学积分比较竞争有序态的自由能,验证最宽的两个相区; 4) 研究双层结构对相稳定性的抑制效应。

Result: 1) 量子涨落在g²阶选择⟨3⟩(↑↑↑↓↓↓)相,因为畴壁附近的自旋通过虚翻转获得最多能量; 2) 调制相⟨23⟩、⟨223⟩、⟨2223⟩依次在g⁴、g⁶、g⁸阶于⟨3⟩和反相⟨2⟩之间出现,遵循经典三维模型的Fisher-Selke序列; 3) 第二层对每个连续相的抑制作用递增,超出局域场硬化效应; 4) 量子蒙特卡罗模拟将⟨3⟩相楔形区重现至约1%精度,在预注册测试中⟨23⟩相在七个测试点中的六个点稳定,第七点通过事后运行得到解决。

Conclusion: 在横场ANNNI模型的经典多相点,量子涨落系统地按Fisher-Selke序列选择和稳定特定的调制相结构。微扰理论预测与量子蒙特卡罗模拟高度一致,证实了量子效应在竞争相互作用系统中打破简并的机制。双层结构增强了对高阶调制相的抑制。该研究为理解量子涨落在阻挫磁性系统中的相选择提供了定量理论框架。

Abstract: We study the transverse-field axial next-nearest-neighbor Ising (ANNNI) model on the square lattice and on a bilayer. At the classical multiphase point $κ=J_2/J_0=1/2$, every configuration of straight domain walls that separates domains of at least two sites is a ground state. The walls are rigid, so perturbation theory in the transverse field $g=Γ/J_0$ can be carried out for each configuration separately, and we take it to order $g^8$. Quantum fluctuations select $\langle 3\rangle$ ($\uparrow\uparrow\uparrow\downarrow\downarrow\downarrow$) at order $g^2$, because spins next to a wall gain the most energy from virtual flips. The structures $\langle 23\rangle$, $\langle 223\rangle$ and $\langle 2223\rangle$ then open between $\langle 3\rangle$ and the antiphase $\langle 2\rangle$ at orders $g^4$, $g^6$ and $g^8$, in the order of the Fisher-Selke sequence of the classical three-dimensional model. A second layer suppresses each successive phase more strongly, beyond the stiffening of the local fields. We test the two widest phases with stochastic series expansion quantum Monte Carlo, comparing the free energies of the competing ordered states by thermodynamic integration, since annealing freezes in the modulation that forms at the ordering transition. The simulations reproduce the $\langle 3\rangle$ wedge to about 1%, and in a pre-registered test $\langle 23\rangle$ is stable at six of seven points, with the seventh resolved by post-hoc runs.

</details>
